# FS25 vault capability audit

Status: **in progress**, last updated 8 November 2026. This audit reopens the question that the 65-entry companion validation matrix could not answer: whether every vault mod with useful, distinct data has an honest Farm Dashboard reading. The completed matrix is evidence for its tested integrations, not a vault-wide pass.

## Inventory boundary

- Current read-only `P:\FS25 MOD VAULT\by-category` enumeration: **8,012 ZIP paths**. The maintained vault index dated 26 October lists 8,011, so it is not an exact current inventory.
- The 23 September source-screening inventory covered **665 ZIP names**. By filename, 602 remain in the current category tree, 63 are absent, and 7,407 distinct current names were outside that older sample. Filename overlap is a triage aid, not proof of identical bytes or versions.
- The read-only `2026-10-31-scan-vault.py` scan covered all 8,012 current category-tree ZIP paths. **1,803** contain Lua and **1,549** declare source files in `modDesc.xml`. **1,201** Lua-bearing ZIP names were outside the older 665-name screen. The full machine-readable result is `Z:\Graham\agent-comms\farmhub-companion-acceptance\VAULT-SCAN-2026-10-31.json`.
- `FS25_Bale_Storage.zip` in `uncategorized` was unreadable as a ZIP; its contents and playability are unproved. No vault files were modified.
- A title/category/source-file keyword pass put **118 ZIPs across 91 normalized titles** into an initial review queue. The queue includes duplicate versions, RF packages, native-data overlap and false positives; **118 is not a missing-integration count**. The sortable 1,201-row Lua triage file is `Z:\Graham\agent-comms\farmhub-companion-acceptance\VAULT-LUA-TRIAGE-2026-10-31.csv`. Every row is still a source-review lead, not an acceptance result. Lower-scoring rows must also be assessed before claiming the vault audit exhaustive.
- The repeatable `2026-11-08-vault-review-queue.py` compares exact archive names in the 7 November 1,803-row Lua-signal snapshot with this audit and prints current counts at configurable score thresholds. These are **name-mention gaps only**, not missing-integration counts or proof that the other paths were source-reviewed. Maps, RF-owned packages, duplicate versions and control-only mods appear in the unmentioned queue. The check is used to choose the next source review without treating a textual mention as acceptance.

## Decision rule

For each candidate, establish the exact current ZIP hash/version and source-owned state; decide whether that state is distinct from the normal game export, farm-scoped and safely readable; add a bounded collector and visible reading where justified; then compare a fresh nonempty game state, committed export and installed page. Classify native-game overlap and detection-only mods honestly. Never call an empty, unavailable or settings-only result a gameplay pass. RF-owned Ally work has priority; no direct Graham-PC UI use, public release, main push or RF/VPS changes are in scope.

## Additional lower-ranked source triage, 7 November

- Easy Logging v1.0.2.0 (`gameplay/FS25_361940_FS25_EasyLogging.zip`, SHA-256 `39EBADA306CCD16FD6259B4AB5613042C8A0B3CC574A30E2B7D37F7575DB6EC6`) stores its cutting presets and assist settings under profile-wide `modSettings/FS25_EasyLogging/settings.xml`. The `farmId` appearing in event-driven cut metadata is a runtime work context, not a persistent, farm-keyed timber ledger. No distinct farm-owned historical reading is established from the exact source; classify as control/settings only unless a separate persistent record is found.
- Manual Hydraulic Control v1.1.0.0 (`gameplay/FS25_367607_FS25_ManualHydraulicControl.zip`, SHA-256 `8468CB9D5354C9D4A36B5D11D7F25570F8ECB31E6ABE5F24706AA4D7B96C5B62`) attaches a vehicle specialization for manual joint movement. Its `lastLowered` and held-input state are runtime control state, with no mod-owned save ledger in the reviewed Lua. The `onLoad(savegame)` parameter and `loadFromSavegame` branch are not themselves evidence of a persistent farm metric. Detection/control only; no data page justified here.
- Grass Windrow Merger v1.0.0.0 (`gameplay/FS25_373903_Grass_Windrow_Merger.zip`, SHA-256 `F014FFF729CBFD72221619CE80B12DC81429ED1D9C60E709A3E61F244E1060D6`) overwrites mower-area processing and accumulates `workArea.lastPickupLiters` during the operation. The exact two-Lua-file archive has no separate saved farm ledger; this immediate working value is not a durable crop yield or storage balance. Detection only.
- Multiplayer Achievements - Per Player v1.0.0.0 (`gameplay/FS25_374265_Multiplayer_Achievements_-_Per_Player.zip`, SHA-256 `7FD0AE6E0EC4D163B890D60C1C6B128032481FBA1F022113634A1EE372BF5F89`) has two different scopes. `modSettings/FS25_MultiplayerAchievements/progress.xml` is **personal profile** progress and must not be exported as a farm reading. Separately, `MPAchievementShared` stores a `mpaLedger` on each `Farm` in the save XML: eligible clean-money balance/peak and cow, sheep, pig and chicken breeding counts. Its hooks and snapshots run only when the mission is multiplayer, game-started and server-owned. This is a genuine farm-keyed candidate distinct from the current bank balance, because the mod deliberately excludes cheat/unsupported income and ineligible breeding. A source-tested read-only collector/page now require active, game-started server multiplayer, exact export farm and an existing ledger; they read the fields without calling `getLedger` (which initializes/clamps state), apply its non-mutating balance clamp and label values as eligibility counters rather than bank balance or realized profit. Focused exact-farm, foreign-farm, malformed-ledger, single-player/client and UI tests passed, as did typecheck and Vite build. No nonempty multiplayer game/export/installed proof or private package exists yet; a single-player Ally save cannot validate this path, and Ally is currently blocked at StockGuard's DLC-version gate.

This list mixes accepted bounded readings, source-tested readers and unresolved leads. Each row's status states its evidence limit; the table is not a vault-wide pass. Further source checks may add, remove or reprioritise candidates.

Implement Usage Counter private candidate: V5 5.2.27 installer SHA-256 `D74799CCB87A4F94C14156E61A8404397E80251086496310216D3B36304BB087`, exporter 5.1.0.20 ZIP SHA-256 `20C52FF73A9CF20B41ED9790C3E8F58D0AA4193C88A2A254F2B5AEEE4DE02662`, and the exact v1.2.0.0 vault ZIP SHA-256 `B7916A6407795B7A3DE1D1175F0FD455B6A7E2014D7355339859DB33E8DDE4F2` are staged under `Z:\Graham\agent-comms\farmhub-companion-acceptance\implement-usage-candidate`. The exporter archive has 98 safe, unique entries and a matching runtime/descriptor version. Focused collector and merge tests, Vite build, typecheck and locale verification passed. The frozen V3 packet SHA-256 `F5DC9400AEA2C00833CC504049E14773FEB64A7A862F2430AB15C47754859CE6` returned **PARTIAL**. The installed V5 5.2.27 page, committed generation 7 export and mod-loaded game showed 13 owned implements, 14 area counters, one bale counter and zero used counters; all 15 rows were farm 1 with 0.00 ha or 0 bales. No supported implement was hitched to a controllable vehicle, so Tina could not observe the mod HUD or safely make a working pass. This proves a zero-state, owned-row/unit data path only; nonzero game/export/UI agreement, accrual, reset and hide-on-uncheck remain untested. I independently rehashed the exact packet, installer, exporter and third-party ZIP; reviewed active marker, decoded export, installed-page screenshots, load lines and restoration readback. StockGuard loaded without refusal, one Windows row and eight generations were reported; no Lua stack appeared, though separate texture/console/mission errors did. Ally was restored by baseline hashes. A zero-initialized counter or native operating hours cannot establish worked area or wrapped-bale behavior.

Bale Batch Tracker private candidate: V5 5.2.28 installer SHA-256 `8E91F490B7E892D67B9FD735C3F570E30103CC3E5F3D37E073B392D531FF9806`, exporter 5.1.0.21 ZIP SHA-256 `02E0CAD011F956D145BE18A15CC7E5602A97ED461DA026DE409741A25E536060` and exact tracker ZIP SHA-256 `C614FF07E8924D9666A731A12DD07B8F7FDEE9DA36C643275C171B16722B3EE6` are staged under `Z:\Graham\agent-comms\farmhub-companion-acceptance\bale-batch-candidate`. The exporter archive has 99 safe, unique entries with matching runtime/descriptor version. The collector accepts only a mod snapshot whose `farmId` exactly equals the export farm and never copies the mod's bale-object keys or progress-value arrays. Source test rejects a shared global and an unknown/wrong farm; UI typecheck, Vite build and 27-locale coverage passed. Frozen V4 packet SHA-256 `F47CF50C92DD7A37AC31117BA7458BB176DFF601ACD7E93580BA3505D8B10CC0` returned a bounded **PASS** for nonempty grouped bale state with normal StockGuard selected. I independently rehashed the packet and all three exact artifacts, reviewed the mod HUD and installed page screenshots, decoded export slots, active markers, log and restoration readback. The game panel and committed generations 6/7 showed 355 tracked bales (126 physical, 229 stored), 18 ready silage bales, zero fermenting and four named fill-type groups; hay/storage rows and rounded litres matched. The installed V5 page matched later generations 8/9: the same counts/states, 94,502 L ready silage and 882,700 L straw. Straw had been 882,893 L in the earlier game panel and generations 6/7; the 193 L change occurred before the later export, and no later game-panel reading was captured. Thus exact three-way straw volume at one instant is unproved, while each measured game/export and export/page pair agreed. No in-progress fermentation, foreign-farm bale, hide-on-uncheck or bale movement was tested. One Windows row, nine generations, one target/FarmDash/StockGuard load line each and no Lua stack were observed; separate texture, console and mission errors appeared. Tina reported full Ally hash restoration.
Vehicle Inspection HU V2 was privately packaged as V5 5.2.26 installer SHA-256 `C44F866FE17E9D8A233C283D814D026C43DD8CCAD8FC919C8859BBBD8C031C7A` and exporter 5.1.0.18 ZIP SHA-256 `C8CC8E83CFE406A9C7B58BB583AD253407E1EA7E53CE0D399CF9711F6F580537`, staged with the exact HU ZIP under `Z:\Graham\agent-comms\farmhub-companion-acceptance\vehicle-inspection-hu-candidate`. The bounded `FARMHUB-ALLY-20261031-VAULT-V2` packet SHA-256 `727A1A5D437FD4D368FA607904B592C172366F04CC34F905B41D9E4C9D600A76` returned a substantive **FAIL**. The HU game tab showed 41 inspection vehicles, including the farm's John Deere 6R 250 at 12 months remaining/Valid; committed exports in two runs showed only `readState=settings-only`, `reason=no-inspection-register`, and the installed full page reflected that empty export. The normal StockGuard build accepted HU and the measured logs showed no Lua stack. The original collector used `ctx.global("g_huManager")`, while the exact HU `HUMain:loadMap` writes that name in HU's own mod environment. The collector now reads the active mod environment via `ctx.symbol`, with a `HUMain.manager` fallback. A focused Fengari test reproduces a populated mod-local register and rejects a shadow shared-engine register. The subsequent V2R1 installed retest passed this corrected read path. Tina reported full Ally hash restoration after V2; no public release was made.

The corrected exporter 5.1.0.19 ZIP SHA-256 `01FE77ACA88E7314F42A956732EA5F409F725DB5012A15E02DF1781C4E96EF63` is privately staged under `Z:\Graham\agent-comms\farmhub-companion-acceptance\vehicle-inspection-hu-retest`; it has 98 ZIP members, no duplicate or unsafe names, the corrected collector, and matching `modDesc.xml` version. The unchanged V5 5.2.26 installer and exact HU ZIP were independently rehashed. Frozen packet `TEST-PACKET-V2R1.md` SHA-256 `15941098D96433046C7FDA85BBC0A5DEFB00EB0F100CF031E21A2E6E1EFA68F8` returned **PASS for the HU register reading path** with normal StockGuard selected. I independently rehashed all four artifacts, checked the committed active marker (generation 7, slot b, sequence 8), decoded export, game and installed-page screenshots, log load lines and restoration readback. The game register showed 41 vehicles across farms; the export reported 35 farm-1 vehicles and exactly 35 farm-1 rows, excluding the six Farm 100 rows. Its John Deere 6R 250 row has a 12-month interval and 12 months remaining; the installed page showed 35 in register, zero due/overdue/decommissioned, and the same 12-month reading. The measured log contains one load line each for HU, Farm Dashboard and StockGuard, ten committed generations and no Lua error. Tina's full profile/mod/save/shortcut restoration readback matched the recorded baseline. The newly registered full intervals prove the **read path**, not a month-countdown, due/overdue transition, performed inspection, custom interval or hide-on-uncheck.

The first private test candidate is V5 5.2.25 installer SHA-256 `8B36AC3A05C095541D2B5886329DD28FC159A078B98FA5EB86F702CC2BF4A67B` plus exporter mod 5.1.0.17 SHA-256 `BEB286136103AFC9F06B5F82D1DFE51D5870BBA8C9A0F6942A7EE27F3DF02703`, staged under `Z:\Graham\agent-comms\farmhub-companion-acceptance\production-control-candidate`. The mod ZIP has 96 members, no duplicate or backslash entry names, and the collector is listed in `modDesc.xml`. The bounded `FARMHUB-ALLY-20261031-VAULT-V1` packet SHA-256 `E13BD9E6B5BE0F7ED5E16C2DD7CCF9EBD1CED667C26F49168773ACB882A9A299` received a substantive result at 20:01 UTC. I rehashed the frozen packet, staged installer, staged mod ZIP and exact vault Production Control ZIP; all match their specified hashes. On a disposable unsaved save, the game displayed Small Glass Greenhouse Chili Peppers changing from 100%/528 cycles/£2 to 150%/792 cycles/£3 while six other lines remained 100%. Committed export generation 9 reported farm 1, seven lines, one changed, Chili `baseCyclesPerMonth=528`, `cyclesPerMonth=792`, `baseCostPerMonth=2.4`, `costPerMonth=3.6`, all exact 1.5 ratios; the installed full page showed the same rounded readings. Unchecking Production Control removed its load line, export page and app link. One Windows row, committed generations and no Lua stack in the measured run were reported; Tina's full hash restoration readback matched the as-found Ally baseline. This is a **StockGuard-off configured-rate pass** only: the first load with normal StockGuard stopped at `UNBOUND_ADAPTER (FS25_ProductionControl)` before any export. StockGuard is RF-owned and was not modified; a combined normal-set acceptance requires an RF-owned compatibility decision and a fresh bounded test. The chain stayed inactive, so realized throughput/cost and MP paths remain untested. A momentary app `Stale` badge was observed but no wrong page value established.

Native-overlap examples need a different decision: `Statistics Extended` v1.0.0.1 (`FS25_364343_FS25_statsExtended.zip`, SHA-256 `EE6C655FE2E61D2DC0A19B14884AC316C802F106C1729D85A31095BA2450643C`) reads `farm.stats.statistics` session and total counters and `farm.stats:getTotalValue()` achievement progress for the local farm when its in-game tab opens. Its five Lua files have no savegame XML writer or new counter accumulator; the package is a presentation of native statistics, not a distinct mod-owned ledger. `Animal Storage Overview` reads standard husbandry storage/food values to extend the in-game Animals frame. Those can motivate better generic Farm Dashboard views, but their source does not yet establish unique mod-owned state requiring an optional collector. `Ingame Time Operating Hours` changes the game's effective hour/fuel behaviour; avoid counting a second copy of native hours as a new mod-specific metric.

Two high-ranked vault leads are **already source-integrated but lack a meaningful nonempty installed pass**. Field Profitability Ledger v1.0.1.0 (`FS25_369866_FS25_FieldProfitabilityLedger.zip`, SHA-256 `F4A832326835C42D4454376250D6997427C6635A1F7030432684AB0342D80B32`) exposes its single-player `Runtime.SinglePlayerRuntime.listCycles({farmId=...})` and `queryCycle` methods. FarmHub's existing farm-filtered reader queries up to eight cycle headers and three summaries, and the catalog accurately says single-player only. The historical B2 matrix marked its reader unavailable, so a nonempty field-cycle game/export/UI comparison is still needed. The uncategorized `KM_80685_Field_Profitability_Ledger.zip` is byte-identical; the GitHub-named v1.0.0.0 archive is an older distinct version. Farmland Marketplace v1.0.5.0 (`FS25_369241_FS25_FarmlandMarketplace.zip`, SHA-256 `331588DE99894BB62FB8EC5D43B28F8B29C64CE6674C204E6A6C4C943DAF7DB1`) exposes `FlaechenVermarktung.manager.state` with sale offers, lease offers and leases. FarmHub already filters those rows to offers/contracts touching the export farm, but B2 reported unavailable; a live offer or lease on a disposable save is needed before claiming meaningful value. These are acceptance gaps, not new missing adapters.

Dependency packages must be tested together, without silently requiring unrelated mods.

Further source checks for the next candidates:

- Field To-Do List v0.1.0.17 (`GH_knoellix__FS25_ToDo_FS25_FieldToDoList.zip`, SHA-256 `D1B428B296F93906AD8A297E07726F3FE7F70C1828452909791B0B8CF798BE18`) persists manual/field tasks with text, completed state, field and optional `farmId` in save-local `fieldToDoList.xml`; planned crops are keyed only by field ID. The current `ToDoManager` exposes `manualTasks` and a farm-filtered method, but that method treats a task with **nil farmId** as belonging to whichever farm asks. On load, migrateOrphanTaskFarmIds assigns each unkeyed legacy task to the then-local farm in memory, so a numeric farm ID in the live register does not prove original ownership. The task reader remains deferred until saved-row provenance can be established. A separate source-tested planned-crop reader maps the mod's engine field ID (farmland ID) or pseudo-field ID (1,000,000 plus farmland ID) to live `g_farmlandManager:getFarmlandOwner` and shows only current export-farm land. It labels records as stored plans that may predate an owner change, never as farm-authored work. These are distinct plans and assignments, not native field work completed; suggestions from the mod's scanner should not be copied as user-created tasks. Focused exact-farm, foreign-land, pseudo-field, unresolved-owner, client and UI tests passed, as did typecheck and Vite build. No task collector, nonempty planned-crop game fixture, committed export or installed comparison exists; Ally remains blocked at StockGuard's DLC-version gate. This package is separate from the already catalogued Task List mod.
- Futures Market v1.2.0.0 (`KM_82100_FUTURES_MARKET.zip`, SHA-256 `737C03BB08AAC585C7430095ECF5F47C9D31AFF825D41017B4FCF949290E5F2C`) saves open long/short commodity contracts with explicit `farmId`, crop, amount, leverage, entry price, posted margin and an expiry index to save-local `futuresMarket.xml`. Its server creates/closes positions and synchronizes the contract list to clients; closing removes the row and posts any payout to native farm money, so the current list is **open positions**, not a realized-profit ledger. A new read-only collector/full page filters the live server list to the exact export farm and reports recorded terms and total posted margin, without calling `getContractPnL`, close, update or money methods. The mod sets `expiration = currentPeriod + term` and compares it with `currentPeriod`; until an actual rollover is observed, the page deliberately labels the raw number a **mod expiry index**, not a guaranteed calendar date. Focused farm-isolation/client-withholding tests, typecheck, Vite build and 27-locale checks passed. This is source-tested only: no private package, nonempty installed game/export/page comparison, margin call or settlement pass.
- Financial Dashboard v2.0.0.0 (`KM_83516_Financial_Dashboard.zip`, SHA-256 `CAB05E086903E5838DF147EDADEE55D5CF937254413C7784B7F785C7DA5025C8`) declares `multiplayer supported="false"`. Its `FinancialDashboard` listener samples the local mission balance, saves at most 500 balance samples and up to 365 daily aggregates to `financialDashboard.xml`, and checks the saved `farmId` against the local player's farm on load. Those dated observations could add a **single-player, local-farm history** reading; they are not a server-wide account ledger. The mod calculates inflows/outflows from differences between sampled balances, explicitly calling them estimates and treating changes while the game was closed as unknown. A future reader must require single-player and exact farm identity, show sample history/estimated movements with freshness, and avoid relabelling differences as classified transactions. No collector, nonempty history or installed comparison exists. The current balance alone duplicates native finance.
- GN Realism - Factory Contracts v1.1.0.0 (`KM_82639_Factory_Contracts.zip`, SHA-256 `60BAD2025EE48F633044FF5E79C9D5F346C57CD4D42FBD501A36347576230627`) stores signed contracts in `g_currentMission.FactoryContracts.contracts`, keyed by contract ID with an explicit `farmId`, destination, product, quantity, locked price, delivery windows and status; it also retains tickets, settlements and supply orders. These are distinct trade obligations rather than native crop stock. Its manager exposes `loadState`, and the server loads the save-local XML before authoritative readings are safe. Although the descriptor declares multiplayer support, `loadMap` still comments that moving the in-memory contract/ticket register to server authority and streams/events is TODO; do not infer multiplayer parity from that descriptor. A first reader would require loaded server state, filter signed contracts to the exact export farm, avoid unsold board offers and avoid mutating validation/build methods. A bounded nonempty contract game/export/installed comparison is needed; no collector or gameplay acceptance exists here.
- Custom Used Market v1.0.0.0 has two byte-identical vault ZIPs (`FS25_374002_Custom_Used_Market.zip` and `KM_82867_Custom_Used_Market.zip`), SHA-256 `23C389144CE970463E3617EDBE06E56CFFA1137683AA3D122926ED05AF7BBB1E`. The authoritative `CustomUsedMarket.settings` contains save-wide offer-generation/discount/duration controls; `offerMonthsLeft` is a persisted per-offer remaining-month map and is copied to each generated native `VehicleSaleSystem` item as `cumMonthsLeft`. The actual vehicle, price and sale listing live in the native used-vehicle system, so duplicating those alone adds no mod-specific reading. Remaining months could add value to a clearly labelled **shared market** view, not the current farm's owned fleet; a reader would need to join only current generated offers by the mod's stable key, avoid stale tracking entries, and prove nonempty offer aging across a period. This is a distinct market-wide candidate, lower priority than farm-owned state, not a farm-scoped missing integration count.
- Base Invoices v1.2.1.0 (`KM_75762_Invoices.zip`, SHA-256 `08930335CCFDE1227AB4935BD98187C8F2D1C405B1CC341B4C13DB74A3E63BFB`) is already integrated through `InvoicesDataCollector.lua` and the app's Invoices section. Its `InvoiceRepository` saves invoice IDs, sender/recipient farm IDs, line amounts, VAT, penalty, date and state to save-local `invoices.xml`; this is distinct from native balance alone. Source review found a v1.2.1.0 `PROPOSED=5` state absent from the existing reader. The mod intentionally presents an unapproved proposal as **incoming to its issuer** and **outgoing from its requester**, whereas the reader had inferred direction from sender/recipient payment IDs and labelled state 5 as unpaid. Source now uses the manager's incoming/outgoing lists, labels proposals separately and excludes them from payable summary totals; a focused Lua proposal test, typecheck, Vite build and 27-locale verification passed. This correction is **source-tested only and is not in the already staged V15 Auto Sell Silo package**. No exact v1.2.1.0 proposed invoice has been compared across game, fresh export and installed page, and payment/penalty gameplay remains untested. The base Invoices package is one existing integration, not another missing collector.

- SIM Dashboard Interface v1.0.0.0 (`FS25_SIMDashboard_Interface.zip`, SHA-256 `208F7E62DCAC98833EBE84C7349DA6486C63ACB1D9555FFCE74C6DD4B17429E6`) describes a game-data bridge to an Android second-screen app. Its only Lua file is a single 160,811-character compressed/obfuscated line; the exact source cannot be safely classified as exposing a distinct farm-owned persisted value by ordinary static inspection. The descriptor alone is insufficient to claim a new farm ledger or to prove the absence of one. Mark this as **opaque, no validated Dashboard reading** pending documented API/runtime evidence; do not count it as an accepted integration or an established missing farm-data adapter.

- Invoice VAT Account v1.0.0.0 has two byte-identical vault ZIPs (`FS25_373914_Invoice_VAT_Account.zip` and `KM_83070_Invoice_VAT_Account.zip`), SHA-256 `2BDD3B75299D20B68DA0C8FCA975C9BF0F898DEB02FCB46BF1DCC5D56232535E`. It depends on `FS25_Invoices`. Its only persisted state is a **save-wide** `recipientFarmId` setting in `invoiceVATAccount.xml`. On a paid invoice it credits that farm's native balance through `MoneyType.INVOICE_INCOME`; it keeps no VAT ledger, per-invoice payout history or mod-owned farm balance. A dashboard could label the configured recipient farm as a global setting, but must not present it as every farm's income or invent a VAT total from the setting. Native finance and the underlying Invoices repository are the appropriate sources for actual transactions. Classify as a settings-only, lower-priority capability unless a separate, source-backed farm reading is established.
- Farm Money Manager v1.0.0.0 has two byte-identical vault ZIPs (`ITCH_3652788_FS25_FarmMoneyManager.zip` and `ITCH_3837958_FS25_FarmMoneyManager.zip`), SHA-256 `F66CB0E22EA7DB15456D8DBFD56CFC16ABEC3FEF5C3F367ABEEDD5519D94CF4D`. Its two Lua files implement an admin-controlled amount entry/console command and a network event. The server validates a farm ID and calls native `g_currentMission:addMoney(amount, farmId, MoneyType.OTHER, ...)`, then prints old/new native balances. It saves no separate ledger, balance, budget or account state. The existing native farm finance reading already covers the balance; detecting this mod as an available money-editing control is honest, but a new Farm Dashboard data page would duplicate native state or invent transaction history. Count the two paths as one capability and classify it as no distinct mod-owned farm reading.
- Invoices - Production Sales v1.0.0.0 has two byte-identical vault ZIPs (`FS25_373258_Invoices_-_Production_Sales.zip` and `KM_82919_Invoices_-_Production_Sales.zip`), SHA-256 `791CECBDD7EF15B6C802DF012EDF3449DD15A941BB8DE9049D564DDC6F88FBDA`. It declares the `FS25_Invoices` dependency (version 1.2 or later). On the server, `g_currentMission.invoicesProductionSalesManager.offers` records `sellerFarmId`, configured price, listed/remaining volume and enable state. `openBatches` have explicit seller and buyer farm IDs and running totals. Both are persisted in the mod's XML. A closer read found that `closedReceipts` is only a reference-to-invoice-ID map: it has **no farm ID** and cannot safely be exported as this farm's sale history without resolving the native Invoices record. Its `buildSnapshot(farmId)` selects owned production outputs, buyer/seller batches and receipts for a requesting farm, but calls `rebuildProductionIndex` and `validateOffers`, so an exporter should read/filter offers and open batches directly without invoking that builder. A farm-owned active product offer with configured price/remaining quantity would add distinct value; a completed sale needs a second farm and the Invoices dependency and remains a separate gameplay test. Do not show the all-farm market as the current farm's sales or assume a single-farm save proves a transfer.
- Farm Limit Manager v1.0.0.0 has two byte-identical vault ZIPs (`FS25_372370_Farm_Limit_Manager.zip` and `KM_82655_Farm_Limit_Manager.zip`), SHA-256 `58C1005A23CB0F9DB6180DFA0C30877A244ADF115DBFBF6B689307A54E1D24B8`. `g_farmLimitManager.settings` holds five **save-wide** caps (active AI workers, husbandries, greenhouses, factories, contracts), defaulting to one each and persisted in `farmLimitManager.xml`; these are not per-farm settings. Its count helpers take a farm ID for active jobs/contracts and owned placeables. A dashboard could show the save-wide caps beside exact export-farm utilization, clearly distinguishing scope, but native counts alone are not a mod-owned capability. The source has no historical limit-hit or denied-purchase ledger. Classify as lower-priority settings/utilization candidate; a nondefault cap and a real blocked action would be needed to establish behavior beyond static defaults.
- Bale Batch Tracker builds `BaleBatchTracker.snapshot` every 5 seconds (every 1 second with fermenting bales). Its two vault paths (`FS25_374533_Bale_Batch_Tracker.zip` and `KM_83075_Bale_Batch_Tracker.zip`) have identical SHA-256 `C614FF07E8924D9666A731A12DD07B8F7FDEE9DA36C643275C171B16722B3EE6` and are one package. The snapshot includes physical/stored counts and litres, fermentation groups, ready counts and storage rows. Its source takes `g_currentMission:getFarmId()` as the snapshot farm. A collector must require `snapshot.farmId == exportFarmId`; if the mod has no farm ID on a dedicated server, it must report unavailable rather than export an all-farms aggregate. The normal dashboard already has a bale inventory, so the extra value is the mod's grouped fermentation and readiness view, not duplicate totals.
  A second read of the exact Lua confirms this snapshot is rebuilt live by `update`/`refresh`, not a persisted historical ledger. When `getFarmId()` is absent or resolves to spectator, `activeFarmId` is nil and both the loose-bale and object-storage scans deliberately accept every farm. The snapshot also contains live bale-object keys in `fermentingBales` that must never be serialized into Farm Dashboard. The **existing** collector requires exact snapshot/export farm ID and copies capped scalar groups; V4 already passed one bounded nonempty game-panel/export/installed-page reading. Headless/spectator data, an in-progress fermentation change and foreign-farm isolation in game remain unaccepted.
- Net Worth Tracker records `g_nwt_historyManager.histories` on `DAY_CHANGED` and persists those samples. The exact `NWT_historyManager.lua` has one category (`total`) and calls `getFarmByUserId(g_currentMission.playerUserId).farmId` without a nil guard, so it records only the local player's farm on that event; it does not iterate all farms. Dedicated-server behavior is unproved and may be unavailable. A read-only collector/page now reads the mod-local manager, filters `total` samples by the exact export farm ID, caps rows, and shows dated amounts separately from Farm Dashboard's current net-worth estimate. It does not call `recordFarmValue`, manufacture historical points or imply that an absent row means zero worth. A focused Lua/merge test proves nonempty farm isolation and rejects a shared-global shadow; UI typecheck and 27-locale key checks passed. The mod's value calculation includes money, debt, equipment, land, placeables, livestock and stored fill, which may differ from Farm Dashboard's current estimate. V6 established one nonempty single-farm amount reading; other farms and day changes remain untested.
- Net Worth Tracker private candidate is staged under `Z:\Graham\agent-comms\farmhub-companion-acceptance\net-worth-candidate`: V5 5.2.30 installer SHA-256 `FCB8D7A27FD1D5E9AA4E198AB099A3647E6294A988D9F45E46AC0A18743D7133`, exporter 5.1.0.23 ZIP SHA-256 `69DF68FE09C62F675F1A5D04352D4198213C0A33C21FD241F3D78B4443A6DEEA`, and exact third-party v1.1.0.3 ZIP SHA-256 `00A27FC7A8A9A239DC19DE411AF89B28B9F9EE385DCA28D577D4FDE100B974E9`. The exporter archive has 102 safe, unique entries with matching descriptor/runtime versions; it also contains the source-tested Harvest Ledger collector, which should remain unchecked in the Net Worth test. - Frozen `TEST-PACKET-V6.md` SHA-256 `2C1FA03206E8E61ADE3817769404CA6A2CA2E2DF953CABECDAC497D021F3EF53` ran as `FARMHUB-ALLY-20261101-VAULT-V6` on a disposable unsaved save. The game history was empty before the day rollover and gained one row, `August 1, 2026 | 13,241,476 GBP`, afterward. Active slot a, generation 12, exported one farm-1 `total` row `{dayId:19, year:2, period:7, dayInPeriod:1, amount:13241476.143938}`; slot b, generation 11, remained empty. The installed 5.2.30 full page showed one recorded day and `13,241,476` for game day 19, year/period/day `2/7/1`. I independently rehashed the exact packet, installer, exporter and third-party ZIP, decoded both export slots, checked their payload byte lengths and Adler-32 fields, inspected the game/page screenshots and log, and read the full Ally restoration report. The amount agrees to the nearest pound across game, export and page; the before/after state proves a day-change capture. The game renders `August 1` while the export/page use numeric period `7`. The exact third-party GUI calls `g_i18n:formatPeriod(((periodId-1) % 12), false)` while its history writer copies `environment.currentPeriod`; the engine's period-to-month mapping was not independently established here, so do not claim a date-label defect or exact date-label three-way agreement. Tina reported one Windows row, 12 generations, StockGuard READY 79 and complete baseline-hash restoration of game/profile/mods/settings/app/shortcuts. The only rollover Lua stack was in `FS25_RedTape` (`TaxStatementPaidEvent.lua:38`), not Net Worth Tracker or Farm Dashboard. Hide-on-uncheck, multiple samples, other farms and dedicated-server behavior remain untested. This is a bounded nonempty amount-reading pass, not vault-wide acceptance or a public release.
- The gameplay `FS25_339475_FS25_netWorthTracker.zip` and uncategorized `KM_72515_Net_Worth_Tracker.zip` are byte-identical v1.1.0.3 archives, SHA-256 `00A27FC7A8A9A239DC19DE411AF89B28B9F9EE385DCA28D577D4FDE100B974E9`. The uncategorized `GH_EvanKirsch__fs25-nwt_FS25_NetWorthTracker_update.zip` is also v1.1.0.3 with identical non-`modDesc.xml` member bytes but archive SHA-256 `EFFF67FE5E1068AADE475D52245209FF9E3ACF44BF0314B18D5FC250DCF496D1`; its description/schema metadata differs, not its Lua behavior. - SWU Net Worth Tracker v1.0.0.1 (`ITCH_4632292_FS25_SWUNetWorthTracker.zip`, SHA-256 `408C3048ADC749AF9819CD4BF9E5F3E49639AF4DE57F2BA9CF33F1EF4BF4BACF`) is a separate implementation. It stores current asset/debt components and a bounded timestamped history in `g_currentMission.netWorthSystem`, persisted through its config module. The history rows have **no farm ID**; the calculation chooses a farm through the local player, user ID 1, then a fallback to the first found farm. Reading this global as the export farm would be unsafe in multiplayer and may be wrong even in a multi-farm save. Do not merge it with Evan Kirsch's farm-keyed history or expose it as farm-scoped without a reliable runtime identity check. The `src/NWT_historicalData.lua` file is only a placeholder; the actual history writer is `scripts/main.lua`.
- Productions Manager keeps `ProductionScheduleManager.data[pointKey][productionId] = { months, scheduleEnabled }` and enforces the schedule on period change. The source actually loads/saves this table at the **profile-wide** `modSettings/ProductionSettings.xml`, despite its comment calling it savegame XML; entries contain no save or farm ID. A safe reader must resolve each stored key and production ID to a *currently existing* production point and line owned by the export farm (the source prefers owning-placeable unique ID, then numeric ID aliases), omit unresolved/orphan entries, and never call `rebuildRuntimeMap`, `applyAll`, or `commitFromUI`. Even a live key match cannot prove that a profile-wide entry was originally configured in this save, so installed acceptance must disclose that identity limit and use a newly set, named nondefault schedule on a disposable save. The scheduled months are distinct from a line's current on/off state. Empty/default schedules are detection only, and a period rollover would be needed to prove enforcement.
- Market Price Helper's actual runtime object is `g_priceTicker`. It persists sampled per-fill-type prices with day/hour/period in `history` and personal alarm choices. The exact `PriceTicker:getConfigFilename()` selects savegame `priceTicker.xml` for a local client but profile-wide `modSettings/FS25_PriceTicker/priceTicker.xml` for a remote multiplayer client; neither record carries a farm ID. The source creates the instance only when `g_client` exists, so a headless dedicated server can have the mod loaded but no readable history object. Its stock routine returns all farms if no current farm ID is available. The `KM_79555_Market_Price_Helper.zip` and `FS25_362332_Market_Price_Helper.zip` vault paths are byte-identical (SHA-256 above), so count one package. A future reading would need explicit local-client and save/farm identity, a nondefault historical sample or alarm, and an unavailable state on a headless server. Native current sell prices, all-farm stock and an unscoped remote-client profile must not be presented as new farm-owned data. No collector or installed acceptance exists.
- VAT System v1.0.0.0 has two byte-identical vault paths (`FS25_373913_VAT_System.zip`, `KM_82789_VAT_System.zip`), SHA-256 `2D58B9A35EFA3D45808B5F815460970FC756C99E5BEEBEFAC93BB463D84B38DF`. The source registers `MoneyType.VAT` and posts VAT transfers through `g_currentMission:addMoney`, so realized tax belongs to farm finance data. A page could label the current save-wide rates and the chosen recipient, but must not manufacture an accrued balance from settings or duplicate native finance entries as mod-owned history. Detection/default settings are not a meaningful nonempty acceptance case.
- Invoice VAT Account v1.0.0.0 has two byte-identical vault paths (`FS25_373914_Invoice_VAT_Account.zip`, `KM_83070_Invoice_VAT_Account.zip`), SHA-256 `2BDD3B75299D20B68DA0C8FCA975C9BF0F898DEB02FCB46BF1DCC5D56232535E`. A real paid-invoice transition would be needed to validate behavior; this exact package has not been installed-tested here.
- Silage Compaction Monitor v1.2.0.0 has two byte-identical vault paths (`FS25_349039_FS25_Silage_Compaction_Monitor.zip`, `KM_75339_Silage_Compaction_Monitor.zip`), SHA-256 `B25665D1DB4B8D6D599226AE895689671D019A4796F047D1A7462F13C2D627C7`. - Farm Reputation 1.1.0.2 (`FS25_367320_FS25_FarmReputation.zip`, SHA-256 `4E72D048EE78C7B620700027530C05EDE78EE1DDBABAA002FBB329A1E1CC2488`) stores `ReputationManager.reputationByFarm`, `contractorStatsByFarm` and `contractorDataByFarm` under numeric farm IDs in save-local `farmReputation.xml`. - Price Alert v1.1.0.0 (`KM_78451_Price_Alert.zip`, SHA-256 `445FB910A3BFEA3BFFBC59341FF473AC34619163583DAC02A3549E5B8AFC3F7E`) is a separate mod from Market Price Helper. `PriceAlertFrame.collectRawData()` gathers native current prices/stock/animals using `g_currentMission:getFarmId()`; the mod-owned `PriceAlertConfig.data` holds configured price-percent and minimum-quantity alert thresholds, and `horsePrices` stores the previous hourly horse price for comparison. - Productions Manager v1.0.0.1 persists `ProductionScheduleManager.data[pointKey][productionId]` with an enabled flag and 1–12 selected periods in `getUserProfileAppPath()/modSettings/ProductionSettings.xml`. Despite the source header calling it savegame XML, the actual path is **profile-wide**. `rebuildRuntimeMap` joins keys to current production points, including legacy ID aliases, but the schedule map itself has no farm ID and may retain entries for other saves or former owners. The new read-only collector iterates `getProductionPointsForFarmId(exportFarmId)`, verifies each point's current `ownerFarmId`, requires the mod's runtime map to refer to that same point object, and reads only recipe IDs present on that point. It never exports the raw profile schedule table or invokes `applyAll`, `save` or `commitFromUI`. ## Contract Manager source lead

Contract Manager v1.26.0.0 (`GH_Dostlar-Studio__FS25_ContractManager_FS25_ContractManager.zip`, SHA-256 `DEA8F23B964992503CE42AADB05C1C2A05D42B2E8D277A7A6DC0BF3433FBBA1F`) has a separate save-local `FS25_ContractManager.xml`. Its server `ContractManagerRegistry` stores active contract metadata, completed/failed/cancelled/timed-out history, per-farm earnings and penalties, and per-farm quota counters. `peekFarmStats(farmId)` and `getHistory(farmId)` are read-only; `getFarmStats(farmId)` creates a missing row and must not be used by a dashboard reader. `ContractManagerPartnership.list` also holds mission-keyed, farm-keyed contribution records. This is distinct farm-owned outcome data; the mod's offer reward does not equal a recorded payout until `onMissionDismissed` fills `entry.payout`.

The first read-only Contract Manager collector/full page requires the active mod and server register, filters active/history/stats to the exact export farm, shows offered reward separately from a recorded payout, and withholds client-only `remoteHistory` because it may be incomplete. It does not call `getFarmStats` or mutate the registry. Focused Lua/merge tests prove farm isolation, no mutation, offer-versus-payout distinction, client withholding and rejection of a shared shadow register; catalog detection, TypeScript and Vite build passed. English fallback labels are in the translation source; the existing 27-language set is intact. V12 now proves one active row through game/export/installed page, while outcome history and its money remain untested. This ZIP is not equivalent to the separate Crop Contracts forward-sale mod.

Private V12 candidate is staged under `Z:\Graham\agent-comms\farmhub-companion-acceptance\contract-manager-candidate`: V5 5.2.37 installer SHA-256 `1C3C5CA55F0A9FA42AED5D8138570780197DB3C75A14BD51AC3A324F42673095`, exporter 5.1.0.30 ZIP SHA-256 `96477A9F59EADFDCCA1390DEB11EB031FB22C34BFB42732424064968A9847F7E`, and the byte-identical canonical-name Contract Manager ZIP SHA-256 `DEA8F23B964992503CE42AADB05C1C2A05D42B2E8D277A7A6DC0BF3433FBBA1F`. The exporter has 115 safe unique entries and matching descriptor/runtime versions. The V5 private packaging guards passed and preserved the existing latest-rf.yml feed. Frozen `TEST-PACKET-V12.md` SHA-256 `BDA524D70F5955422A605EC5A0F0271235A551011E00FEA44F7885745F4C3596` requests one unsaved, bounded farm-owned contract ledger check, with a GBP 500 cap on an optional clearly presented cancellation. These hashes are candidate identities, not installed acceptance.

Exact request `FARMHUB-ALLY-20261101-VAULT-V12` was appended once to `Z:\Graham\agent-comms\to-tina.md` after V11R1 restoration and a fresh RF-ownership check. Tina returned a substantive **partial** result: one farm-1 Deadwood contract offered GBP 1,125, with a matching active row on the mod's game screen, both final committed export slots (generations 9 and 10), and installed V5 5.2.37. The optional cancel was correctly skipped because the displayed five-tree penalty exceeded the GBP 500 cap. No payout, completion, saved history, quota, partnership or multi-farm gameplay is established. Tina reported one Windows row and full hash restoration to V12 as-found; the pre-V7R1 Desktop shortcut content remains unknown. The log has no FarmDash Lua stack, but Contract Manager shielded several game field-completion errors, so this is not an error-free game run.

V12 also exposed a small export defect: `readState=ok` with an active record still carried `reason=no-farm-record`. The collector used Lua's `hasFarmState and nil or "no-farm-record"` idiom, which always returns the reason. It now clears that reason when farm state exists; a focused assertion and build pass. V25 installed exporter 5.1.0.38 subsequently passed the correction on a nonempty active row. The active row and money interpretation were otherwise sound. Deadwood's native mission reports `fieldId=0` and the mod's saved registry does not hold Farmland 89, so the page correctly leaves Field blank; a farmland attribution is not claimed.

## Production Booster System source lead

The exact vault `KM_82331_Production_Booster_System.zip` is v1.0.0.0, SHA-256 `58D1267C5396C483DC524F43EAE9818C21C7129864CAE3A989C255706F04214C`. Its `PBS_ProductionBoosterSystem.lua` saves per-production cumulative base, actual and extra output, output-by-fill-type totals, booster consumption, cost and active-time statistics on each production point. It also saves input priorities and manual booster-enable choices. These are distinct mod-tracked figures, beyond a native current fill level. The source updates the output counters when an owned production runs; they are not sales, deliveries or realized profit. Static XML booster configuration is not a player's saved choice.

A first read-only collector and full page now take only server statistics for the export farm's currently owned points and list nonzero output-by-fill-type base, actual and extra litres. They do not invoke the mod's `pbsEnsureStatistics` helper, which initialises/mutates tables, or its save and control functions. The collector caps points, lines, output types and rows and excludes foreign-farm points even if the production manager supplies them. It deliberately omits aggregate output across different fill types, costs, priority settings and booster consumption from this first view. Focused source-shape tests cover a nonempty owned line, foreign exclusion and client-only withholding. **This is source-tested only**: no game run, positive production, committed export or installed-page comparison exists. Production Booster System's coexistence with the normal RF-owned StockGuard set is unproved; the Production Control refusal does not establish this mod's outcome. A meaningful installed test needs a disposable owned production that runs with the exact mod, a nonzero recorded output and matching game/mod statistic, export and installed page.

## Dynamic Dealership and Advanced Seeding Control source leads

Dynamic Dealership v2.0.0.0 has two byte-identical vault paths, `gameplay/FS25_369544_Dynamic_Dealership.zip` and `uncategorized/KM_80444_Dynamic_Dealership.zip`, SHA-256 `392DBDC07ED5B5124F596984C88A711293FDFA2836E9DD59B8FB7D6A1DF19F76`. Count one package. The exact `main.lua` binds `mission.dealerManager`; its `purchaseOrders` runtime and save-local `dealerInventory.xml` distinguish shared dealer stock orders from farm-keyed CUSTOMER vehicle orders. A customer order retains item XML filename, quantity, delivery day, farm ID, deposit already paid and remaining amount; the source separately handles financed orders. The current dealer inventory and incoming stock are shared market state, not farm-owned. This is a genuine **active-order** lead distinct from native owned vehicles or the separate Vehicle Trade In completed-trade history. A read-only collector and full page now take only the bound server manager's live CUSTOMER orders with an exact export-farm ID and resolved store-item name; they show the mod's order fields, with a clear warning that a financed order's remaining field is not its full loan balance. The reader calls no purchase, delivery or save functions. Focused source-shape tests cover nonempty farm filtering, shared-stock exclusion, foreign-farm exclusion and client withholding. V24 subsequently passed one pending customer-order game/export/installed-page comparison with the normal Witcombe mod selection and StockGuard READY. Delivery, financing, saved persistence, multiple orders and multiplayer remain untested.

Advanced Seeding Control v1.0.0.0 also appears at two byte-identical vault paths, `gameplay/FS25_372286_Advanced_Seeding_Control.zip` and `uncategorized/KM_82871_Advanced_Seeding_Control.zip`, SHA-256 `E3CACC7927D0EA6E70C1CB2C5C7E7B9D077762915BF45840354025A68D1B9C9B`. Its vehicle specialization saves mode, follow-up width, track width, current tramline lane and starter-fertilizer enable on each seeder in native `vehicles.xml`. These are distinct selected implement settings, but its work-area effect changes native field/fertilizer state and it saves no completed tramline or seeded-area ledger. A read-only collector and full page now show the current mode, widths, lane and starter-fertilizer choice on only the export farm's owned seeders. The collector invokes no setting or work function, rejects incomplete states and excludes foreign machines. Focused Lua/merge tests cover nonempty owned Auto and Off settings, a malformed owned state, foreign isolation, client-only withholding and absent mod; typecheck, Vite build and 27-locale verification passed. This remains source-tested only: no owned-machine game comparison, nondefault setting change, work pass, or same-generation installed comparison exists. Two ZIP paths do not imply two missing integrations.

## Vehicle Trade In source lead

Vehicle Trade In v1.0.0.1 (`FS25_362333_FS25_TradeInMenu.zip` and `objects/KM_79798_Vehicle_Trade_In.zip` are byte-identical, SHA-256 `CD8F6BD2189328595536A4BAA2F62FB81F235611437134C310800DA41F01C310`) has a mod-owned `tradeInStatistics.xml` ledger. The exact `TradeInStatistics.lua` retains up to 200 completed dealer transactions, each with farm ID, vehicle name, base appraisal, final trade credit and date; `scripts/main.lua` attaches the runtime as `mission.tradeInMod`, and the mod's Statistics GUI reads that runtime. Completed trade credit is distinct from native current vehicle value. The used-market offer list is shared market state, and financing agreements have separate binding/payment rules; neither is copied by this first reader. A `main.lua` comment says 1.1.0.0, but the packaged `modDesc.xml` declares **1.0.0.1**.

The exact `serverFinalizeTrade` path records a history row only **after** a replacement-vehicle purchase succeeds and the old vehicle is removed. Starting a trade or viewing its appraisal does not create an accepted ledger row; cancelling refunds the temporary credit. A nonempty installed acceptance therefore needs one bounded, completed trade on a disposable unsaved save, with the original vehicle, replacement and credit clearly identified before confirmation. Do not infer completed history from a pending transaction or native vehicle sale.

A read-only collector and full page now take only the exact export farm's completed transaction rows from the server runtime and label the final value as trade credit, not profit. The mod's XML loader substitutes farm 1 for a legacy row with no farm ID before the dashboard can inspect its origin, so the reader withholds farm 1 history in multiplayer rather than risk misattribution. It also withholds client-only or unbound registers and never invokes the mod's mutating save/reset paths. Two focused tests cover nonempty farm isolation, base-appraisal versus final-credit values, no mutation and the multiplayer legacy-owner gate; TypeScript typecheck, Vite build and existing 27-locale verification passed. English fallback copy has 13 new labels. This is **source-tested only**. No completed game trade, same-generation export, installed page, finance payoff, or multiplayer acceptance exists. A future bounded test would require an explicitly approved disposable trade and exact before/after value comparison; an empty ledger alone would not prove value.

- Seasonal Crop Stress & Irrigation Manager appears twice in the high-ranked queue as RF-owned version variants: gameplay `GH_Realistic-Farming__FS25_SeasonalCropStress_FS25_SeasonalCropStress.zip` v1.2.6.1, SHA-256 `94746661F057E36C458672E47EBBD11FB075CB9F8D4BB274BBCF377DA54A2380`, and uncategorized `FS25_SeasonalCropStress.zip` v1.2.5.151, SHA-256 `D897543CB676CD94B598C508FEF9CB748A0B321E1DA3B5516AA200C1CFCDECC2`. These are two builds of one RF-owned capability, not two Farm Dashboard integration gaps. Only package metadata and bytes were checked here; no source or gameplay integration claim follows.
- Storage Overhaul: Pallet Degradation System v1.2.0.0 (`uncategorized/FS25_StorageOverhaulPalletDegradation.zip`, SHA-256 `62CBE66281CD14B04AFDF872A50FBFD27FC5E5AA73779B8E700E7DB81B0E914E`) is an **existing limited integration**, not a new adapter gap. Its manager scans live pallet, big-bag and IBC objects, keys `PalletMetadata.records` by a vehicle unique/savegame/runtime ID, and retains temporary and permanent damage. The save-local `PalletDegradation.xml` has no farm ID and can contain stale records. FarmHub already has a read-only `PalletDegradationCollector.lua` and dedicated page: the collector joins records to live objects and requires `object.ownerFarmId == exportFarmId` before reporting condition, wear and exposure. Its `saleAdjust` is a source-derived estimate; the price modifier exposes no persistent sale or payout ledger. Historical C1/C2 acceptance verified bounded export/UI presence and a single Storage panel, while actual nonempty damage progression, sale adjustment and payout gameplay remain untested. The preceding audit commit incorrectly called this a missing collector and page; this line corrects that classification.

Avatar Switcher v1.0.0.0 (`KM_81711_Avatar_Switcher.zip`, SHA-256 `B9A37BD4A20B6440432D222ECFD42DA41A960269666F602E6C40E28209820E70`) stores named appearance presets in the user's profile `modSettings/FS25_AvatarSwitcher/avatarPresets.xml` and reads or updates `gameSettings.xml`'s `lastPlayerStyle`. The exact `AS_Main.lua` constructs both paths from `getUserProfileAppPath()`, and `AS_XML.lua` reads and writes those profile files. These are player appearance preferences, not a save-local or farm-owned event, balance or operating metric. A per-farm Dashboard collector would misattribute the data; classify this exact version as profile customization rather than a missing farm-data adapter. No game-side appearance switching was tested.

Cultivator Plowing v1.1.0.1 (`KM_82552_Plowing_with_a_cultivator.zip`, SHA-256 `21015148FACBA5DFC28EBD1BEA8D8E19EE141DFD39CC5B2123FB78281E3E7A60`) adds a cultivator shop configuration for cultivation or plowing, injects native work modes where needed, and changes native field plow-level work. The exact specialization derives its selected mode from the vehicle's `configurations["cultivatorPlowMode"]`, defaults its runtime override to nil on load, and resets its limit-to-field switch on post-load; it only streams that switch to clients. The inspected Lua defines no separate saved operation count, completed-work ledger or farm-keyed state. A Dashboard vehicle page could display a currently owned implement's mode as a narrow configuration feature, but the resulting field condition is already native data and a transient runtime override should not be represented as durable progress. No nondefault owned implement or game/export/page comparison exists, so this is a lower-priority vehicle-setting lead, not an accepted meaningful-value integration.

Veterinarian and Animal Diseases uses `g_veterinarian.states` on the server. The exact mod's private husbandry key is `configFileName@x@z` with coordinates rounded to one decimal place; `veterinarian.xml` saves that key, the sick count, disease index and per-animal state without a farm ID. FarmHub's first reader recreates only the mod's key for live husbandries whose `getOwnerFarmId()` exactly matches the export farm. It exports named pen/animal and sick counts plus the disease category when sick, omitting animal names and treatment detail. Missing owned-pen state is marked partial; a client-only snapshot is withheld. Two focused Lua/merge tests, TypeScript typecheck, Vite build and existing locale verification passed. This is **source-tested only**: no positive illness, game treatment, nonempty installed page, multiplayer or save/load proof exists. A healthy zero in a pen is not a treatment pass.

Other medium-ranked source checks:

- Fields Of Stories - Roleplay v1.0.0.5 has two byte-identical vault ZIPs (`FS25_360158_FS25_FieldsOfStories.zip` and `KM_79036_Fields_Of_Stories_-_Roleplay.zip`), SHA-256 `31D09D5528CBE95168DB7D8967D4C35CFCD417553C1839A835ED66BC78A1D638`; count them as one package. Its save-local `IANeighbours_outbound.xml` includes neighbour identities, relationships, relationship scores, situations and a contract-calls-per-day counter, so this is a genuine roleplay-state lead rather than native crop/finance overlap. The source's `IANeighbours:loadMap` and update both stop the mod in multiplayer, despite `<multiplayer supported="true"/>` in the descriptor. Neighbour `farmId` values commonly identify NPC farms and `belongsToFarm` is not enough on its own to assign a relationship to the Dashboard export farm. The outbound XML also includes player position/appearance and save path, which should not be copied into a farm page. A possible reader needs a proved single-player/export-farm identity and a bounded, privacy-conscious selection of relationship or active story progress; no collector, game story event, fresh export or installed page has been validated. This is **unresolved meaningful-value potential**, not an accepted integration or two missing packages.

- Field Contractors v1.5.3.1 (`KM_82386_Field_Contractors.zip`, SHA-256 `38EFD1997FC95CCE307A9E7D44C963A173834AB6175B5182335DE6556B93DB07`) persists its current claims to a **profile settings** file that holds entries for multiple save slots. `FieldContractPersistence:savePersistentContracts` selects a slot key and copies claim records; each saved record includes `contractFarmId`, quoted price, rate per hectare, area and processed area. These are distinct contractor-job progress data, but the profile-wide file must never be read without joining the active save slot and exact farm ID. The save method captures AI course state and mutates persistence fields, so a Dashboard collector must inspect the live claim records read-only and must not call the writer. No collector, named active game job, committed export or installed comparison exists; the presence of fields in XML alone does not prove completed paid work.

- Mowing Contracts Plus v1.0.0.0 (`KM_82704_Mowing_Contracts_Plus.zip`, SHA-256 `90979F5DAB76489C1F165146582966C69DEF4D4E7C5C0144A0BCBCD9008D4E4A`) is a genuine narrow contract-state lead. On single-player server sessions it attaches `mcpChoice` (no offer/pending/accepted/declined) and `mcpReduction` to native `mowMission` objects and saves them under each mowing mission's `mowingContractsPlus` XML node. An accepted offer reduces that contract's reward (source default 50%) in exchange for continued grass-access work; the source's normal offer chance is 25%, with forced offers disabled. A safe reader requires the exact mod loaded, single-player server state, exact `mission.farmId == exportFarmId`, a mowing mission and a recorded choice. It must read the fields directly: `ensureOffer` can create a new choice and must never be called by an exporter. An offered or accepted decision would be distinct from native contract progress, but a game offer, fresh committed export and installed page have not been compared. A default/no-offer contract is detection only; do not claim reduced payment or retained access without completing the gameplay path.

They require the mod's bound symbol, a single-player server mission register and exact assigned farm ID, show only pending/accepted/declined choices with the potential reward reduction, and exclude unrelated or unassigned contracts. They never call `ensureOffer` or report a payout or collected grass. A focused Lua/merge test covers a nonempty accepted and pending decision, foreign-farm exclusion, no offer mutation, multiplayer/client withholding and rejection of an unbound symbol; TypeScript typecheck, Vite build, catalog test and 27-locale verification passed. This remains **source-tested only**. A real assigned offer, game prompt/decision, committed export and installed page comparison are still required before meaningful-value acceptance; the normal 25% offer chance may make a bounded test inconclusive.

That count is a short-lived collect-mode queue reset during wrapping/unloading; it is **not** a lifetime bale total or a property to attribute blindly to the attached baler. A read-only owned-vehicle reader could expose the baler-wrapper mode and toggle when this exact mod is loaded; it should include the host queue only when the current attachment relationship and ownership are proved. No collector, nondefault game setting, fresh export or installed acceptance exists; native `wrappedBales` statistics must not be relabelled as this mod's count.

## Market Dynamics RF-owned futures boundary

The exact `FS25_MarketDynamics.zip` v1.3.1.43 (SHA-256 `D59ADC02B073E399920F1748297B1667A5D6B65046E81DFAF658A99ABF774051`) is RF-owned. Its `MarketSerializer.lua` saves per-fill-type price history and futures contracts in the save; each contract carries a `farmId`, commodity, target/delivered litres, locked price and status. Farm Dashboard already has an RF Market Dynamics aggregate reader, so this is not a new missing-mod page. Source review found that its futures summary copied contracts from all farms and wrote a missing ID into the mod's contract table. Farm Dashboard source now withholds futures until the export farm is resolved, includes only exact-farm contracts and uses a local ID without modifying the RF contract. Focused Lua tests cover farm isolation, unresolved-farm withholding and no mutation. This fix was made after the private V11R1 5.1.0.29 ZIP was packaged, so that candidate does **not** contain it. No installed multi-farm futures acceptance exists, and RF source was not changed. Market-wide events and price movers remain save-wide context rather than farm-owned contract rows.

## Real Land Finance V11 installed evidence and due-date correction

V11 passed a bounded, unsaved Field 76 lease for farm 1. The game showed annual rent GBP 656 and a GBP 383 first debit; both final committed export slots held one owned lease row with raw annual rent 656.758, and installed V5 5.2.35 displayed the same contract and rent at the game's whole-currency precision. The empty baseline was distinctly reported as `settings-only/no-farm-contracts`. I independently rehashed the frozen packet, installer, exporter and exact third-party ZIP, decoded both final export slots, inspected game and installed-page screenshots, and checked the load log and restoration readback. Tina reported one Windows row, committed generations, normal StockGuard READY, no relevant Lua stack and full restoration to the V11 as-found Ally hashes. The older V7R1 Desktop shortcut original bytes remain unrecovered.

V11 also exposed a FarmHub export defect: `monthsToNext=12` while the game showed the next lease rent due in 7 months. Real Land Finance's `FTRManager:getNextDue` calculates a yearly lease from the current calendar period; the collector had copied `contract.monthsToNext`. The collector now calls that read-only method, and a focused 12-to-7 test passes. Private exporter 5.1.0.29 and V5 5.2.36 are source versions awaiting an installed retest. Separately, the game's contract details showed total paid GBP 0 after the GBP 383 debit; that third-party display/state meaning is unresolved and was not exported as a FarmHub payment claim. No save-local mod XML was written because the disposable game was not saved. Payment rollover, arrears, other farms and multiplayer remain untested.

The focused V11R1 private retest is queued with Tina under `FARMHUB-ALLY-20261101-VAULT-V11R1`; ACK is receipt only. ## Lower-ranked source-screen follow-up

- Remaining Bales v1.0.0.0 (`objects/KM_81944_Remaining_Bales.zip`, SHA-256 `8F6638122B4382BE9D6CFC712B35975DC1E4D29DFB8D20D2D8DA53263F0F098E`) adds an in-game HUD estimate for balers/wrappers, not a saved bale ledger. Its `RemainingBalesDisplay.lua` builds row closures at load and calculates `floor(current consumable fill level / selected bale-type usage)` for twine, net or wrap, or minutes of inline-wrap material from the maximum wrap rate. Its specialisation has no save/load hook or farm-keyed counter. This is a **derived current estimate from existing vehicle fill and configuration**, not an extra mod-owned total. A dashboard estimate would need to reproduce the active bale-type/wrapper usage and prove one owned machine's game HUD/export/page parity; treating the display as lifetime bales made would be wrong. Classify this ZIP as source-reviewed derived/native overlap, with no separate collector or installed comparison yet.
- Round Baler Extension v1.2.1.1 appears in `gameplay/FS25_303389_FS25_roundBalerExtension.zip` and `uncategorized/KM_57692_Round_Baler_Extension.zip` as byte-identical 89,944-byte archives, SHA-256 `350390F50A5BEC3464E5F9CBD2A45E2C8E38E0F4251FC413953A75E916838DB3`. This is one capability, not two missing integrations. Unlike the Bale Net/Twine support packages below, it saves a distinct current operating choice on eligible bale wrappers: `spec_toggleableBaleWrapper.currentDropModeState` (1 manual, 2 automatic, 3 collect) and `baleWrapperIsActive` in vehicle XML. Nonstop balers cap the mode at 2. Its tractor-side `spec_roundBalerExtension.wrappedBaleCount` is saved only when positive but resets after dropping/unloading; it is a current collection state, **not** a lifetime bale-production total. A first read-only collector and full Fleet page now require the exact mod, server-side owned-vehicle list, native bale-wrapper specialisation and a valid mod drop mode/boolean wrapper state. They report only currently owned compatible wrappers, their manual/automatic/collect choice and on/off state; they omit the tractor-side transient count. A focused farm-isolation/invalid-state/client-withholding test, UI typecheck, Vite build and 27-locale coverage checks passed. This is **source-tested only**. A private V5 5.2.52 installer SHA-256 E456C915071036F8DFBC336C9F84B4F61D4A3021B87CDB404051CD36B824D6B6 and exporter 5.1.0.40 ZIP SHA-256 21C1E1C2EDC297BB2C90224D2C6A3B7DC6C891ED261E1E722A38BA240A446D48 are staged with the exact third-party ZIP under Z:\Graham\agent-comms\farmhub-companion-acceptance\round-baler-candidate-5.2.52; no installed game/export/page comparison exists yet. Source review does not prove an owned compatible baler or usable game mode in the disposable save.

- Diesel Fuel System v1.0.0.0 (`uncategorized/KM_82984_Diesel_Fuel_System.zip`, SHA-256 `FF520FC82B6DA050A5FD2CF73EF7781B8770C849D9B731AADD36B3B3214DBBC8`) is a distinct, source-backed maintenance and stored-fuel lead. The exact `SCVehicle.lua` attaches to diesel tractors and combines and saves per-vehicle fuel-lot condition, filter obstruction/hours, deposits, trapped water and air/priming state in `vehicles.xml`; `SCPlaceableTank.lua` saves fuel-lot, water, filter and metered-litres state on its placeable tank. `SCPrecio.lua` separately saves a daily diesel-price factor/history for the save, while the game diesel fill type already exposes the resulting current price. A read-only dashboard view could add useful **owned vehicle/tank condition** beyond native fuel litres, but it must select the current export farm's vehicles/placeables, avoid presenting transient calculated effects as completed service, and keep the save-wide price history separate from farm assets. The two Realistic Diesel Price vault ZIP paths are byte-identical SHA-256 `5B5A2FE766344A0CC9C3468E6F03116223AF724949E5C59D91FA7272957179D2` and belong to a separate already catalogued price-preset mod; they are not additional Diesel Fuel System copies. They show the mod's filter hours and obstruction, trapped-water litres, fuel-lot age and oxidation, and explicit air/missing-filter indicators; they do not call the mod's `getSpec` or `scGetFilterState` helpers because those initialise missing state. A focused source-shape/merge test proves a nonzero owned tractor row, foreign/electric exclusion and client withholding; TypeScript typecheck passed. This first view omits the separate placeable tank and save-wide price history. It is **source-tested only**: no nonzero in-game condition, committed export, installed page, multiplayer or coexistence comparison exists, so this is not yet an accepted integration.

- Farm Limit Manager v1.0.0.0 appears in `gameplay/FS25_372370_Farm_Limit_Manager.zip` and `uncategorized/KM_82655_Farm_Limit_Manager.zip`; the two archives are byte-identical, SHA-256 `58C1005A23CB0F9DB6180DFA0C30877A244ADF115DBFBF6B689307A54E1D24B8`, so this is **one** capability. Its only saved mod-owned data is five shared save-wide limits (AI workers, husbandries, greenhouses, factories and contracts, each defaulting to one and clamped to 0–10) in `farmLimitManager.xml`; the file has no farm-keyed result ledger. The manager computes per-farm active job/contract and owned-placeable counts from normal game systems when enforcing those limits, including pending placeable loading data. A remote dashboard could show **current usage versus the shared policy** as a labelled derived reading, but the settings alone are not farm-specific gameplay progress or evidence that a limit has actually blocked an action. No collector, nondefault-setting/game comparison or installed page exists; this is a settings-plus-native-count candidate, not another accepted farm-owned integration.
- FarmKit v1.0.0.3 (`gameplay/GH_NXModdingFS__FS25_FarmKit_FS25_FarmKit.zip`, SHA-256 `64EEABA4BF5D29E72B5205AAF5997D1E9830F61D460098488E150A998974A745`) is one high-ranked field-planning candidate. Its server builds `fieldEntryServerCache` entries keyed by field ID, each with a farm ID, area, fertilizer/lime/herbicide/material rates and total litres; its seed dialog calculates per-crop litres from field area and seed rate. With Precision Farming active it reads live soil/pH/nitrogen/cover maps; without it, the vanilla entry uses spray-type rates and area. These figures are **derived current estimates**, not a persisted record of application or purchases. The separately saved organic-nitrogen mode is save-wide, and the many feature toggles are profile settings rather than farm-owned outcomes. Farm Dashboard already shows field need flags but does not expose this mod's per-material litre planning table. A useful bounded reader would consume only an already populated server cache, require exact current field ownership, label the figures as estimates and record cache freshness/mode; it must not call `getFieldEntriesForNetwork`, which refreshes the cache. Exact source shows the server cache is warmed once per field on update, while later cache refreshes depend on network requests; a cached entry may be stale. The mod's `getFarmIdForField` falls back to the current player farm when a farmland owner is unavailable, so a reader must independently verify the live farmland owner and never trust that fallback for farm isolation. No collector, game/export/page comparison, PF-mode validation or installed acceptance exists yet.

This ZIP path adds no missing collector. A nondefault price-policy gameplay comparison is not established by this source review.

- EnhancedVehicle v1.1.7.1 (`uncategorized/KM_58808_Enhanced_Vehicle.zip`, SHA-256 `9FFEFCA8C8ED1CC20FEF221026C5D83AC645024C40963DCCD0FB4DB46986EA4E`) retains its own per-vehicle odometer and trip meters in `vehicle.vData.is[14]` and `[15]`, with metres added from `lastMovedDistance` while the motor runs. Its save hook persists those readings under each vehicle's `.FS25_EnhancedVehicle.EnhancedVehicle` node. The HUD converts the metres to game distance units through `g_i18n:getDistance` and `getMeasuringUnit`. This is distinct mod-owned state for currently owned vehicles, but it answers the same fleet-distance question as the already accepted Vehicle Mileage fold; do not add the two odometers together or present one as the other's history. A first read-only farm-owned/root-vehicle collector and separately labelled fleet page now read the mod's `vData.is` values without a setter or reset call. A focused collector/merge test covers a positive odometer, trip mode, foreign-vehicle exclusion, absent state and client withholding; UI typecheck and Vite build passed. - Field To-Do List exact source check adds a stricter migration boundary to the earlier candidate note. `ToDoManager:loadFromXMLFile` calls `migrateOrphanTaskFarmIds()`, which assigns every legacy task missing `farmId` to the then-local farm in memory; `taskBelongsToFarm(task, farmId)` also returns true for a nil task farm ID. Therefore filtering the **live** `manualTasks` table by numeric `farmId` alone cannot prove historical ownership after load. A safe multi-farm reading needs original saved-row provenance (or a source migration contract that identifies legacy rows), plus current field ownership for planned crops; creating a normal manual task in a disposable save would test only new-task handling. The task collector remains deferred. The separate planned-crop collector rechecks current farmland ownership, while explicitly leaving plan authorship unproved.

It could show configured dispatch assignments, not actual jobs completed, wages or worker activity.

They require the exact mod, server vehicle state, a non-stock native configuration index matching the mod's live level, and at least one observed raised fill-unit capacity or matching slowed-usage factor. The page separates stock/current litres for raised units from slower-use units whose tank was not enlarged; it does not call either an observed consumption saving or purchase record. Focused Lua/merge assertions cover both effects and exclude stock, foreign, mismatched and client readings; UI typecheck, Vite build and 27-locale verification passed. No game/export/installed comparison, saved persistence or normal-set coexistence proof exists yet. The already frozen V37 Powertrain package predates this source addition and does not include it.

- NX FeedCalculator v1.0.0.3 (`uncategorized/FS25_NXFeedCalculator.zip`, SHA-256 `5E81CDC553A50FB1928A766C63911F91CE757B2CB9022E5C18C43D9ED53B1D69`) is a separate forecast implementation from Weekend Farmers' Animal Food Calculator v1.1.0.2 (`FS25_338159_Animal_Food_Calculator.zip`, SHA-256 `40F815B4343F6460FAF1A05858F39859DEB1B572EEED4EBCFEF0BBABE054C728`). The exact NX source sums each animal cluster's age-indexed food input, optionally applies its Enhanced Animal System lactation factor, multiplies by animal count and planned days per period, and renders monthly litres plus food remaining in the Animals menu. It caches only `daily`, `monthly` and `daysPerMonth` in `AnimalFoodCalculator.data` after an animal-change event, days-per-period change or menu refresh. There is no saved consumption ledger or observed feeding total. The cache is keyed to the husbandry/placeable rather than farm ID and can be stale between refreshes; any future collector must join a current owned husbandry, label the value as an estimate and withhold uncached pens. Both packages define the global `AnimalFoodCalculator`, so simultaneous-load coexistence needs a separate game test before either cache is attributed to one package. The existing Dashboard page targets Weekend Farmers' different cache shape (`inputs`/`outputs` plus scaling), and must not be treated as an NX integration. No NX collector or installed game/export/page proof exists.

- Powertrain Upgrade v1.0.1.0 (`gameplay/FS25_372458_Powertrain_Upgrade.zip`, SHA-256 `0BEEB73022B81B08FFF05D81FB354960966ACAAA34A69E5AD6C9DC43DFB7F974`) adds two native vehicle configuration keys, `ptuPower` and `ptuSpeed`, for motorised shop/workshop vehicles. The exact source generates seven independently chosen levels per key: Stock, +25%, +50%, +100%, +150%, +200% and +500%. It reads the selected indices from each vehicle's native `self.configurations`, treating an absent key as Stock, mirrors them into transient `spec_ptuUpgrade.powerLevel`/`speedLevel`, then applies torque/power and gearing/speed changes when its base motor capture succeeds; top speed is capped at 160 km/h. It has no mod-local save ledger or upgrade-payment history. The selected levels and any verified live mod state must be kept separate from native reported power, top speed and observed driving performance. Do not infer an upgrade from a high native horsepower/speed value alone.

- PTO Load System v1.0.0.0 (`gameplay/FS25_372063_PTO_Load_System.zip`, SHA-256 `4AAEFA588D11BD2817E491F3FAF366A20C8DF534FB0F722D740931EDD44E64FF`) has a **distinct transient calculation lead**, not a saved power or work ledger. Its `PtoLoadModel.calculate` requires a running engine, traverses attached PTO consumers, combines current torque-derived power with configured minimum/harvest estimates, and calculates a requested-versus-available load percentage. `g_ptoLoadSystem.localState` is bound to the locally occupied vehicle and smooths that estimate for the HUD; server `serverStates` tracks overload/cooldown timers per root vehicle. Only HUD position is written to its `FS25_PtoLoadSystem.xml` settings file. A farm page would need a proven read-only server calculation for an owned, currently running PTO combination, and must label requested power/load as a model estimate while keeping actual consumed torque separate. Calling the model from an exporter and its dedicated-server/farm ownership behavior are not yet tested, so no collector or installed acceptance is claimed.

If generation fails it restores the original field state. A successful mission is visible through native contract data already collected by Dashboard; the ZIP has no independent farm-keyed result/history table to display. This source classification does not prove that a generated contract or field restoration works correctly in game, and no gameplay test was run.
