- Storage Warnings (`objects/KM_81400_storage-warnings-storage-and-warehouse-control.zip`, SHA-256 `D942FFA8B02BFA6F55E15F18AE1B68138E8F9BBD879753FA34385E806F861A2B`) is a **derived live-alert candidate**, not a new storage ledger. Its `modDesc.xml` declares v1.0.1.0 while the Lua `SW.VERSION` constant says 1.6.0.1, so identify the integration by exact bytes. `StorageWarnings.alerts` holds sorted construction, animal, production and storage warning rows with severity, percent, object/fill names and optional quantities. `scan()` rebuilds them from current native farm-owned objects, sets `lastScanStats.farmId`, and `update()` runs it only with a local player while the mod is enabled and settings panel closed. Its thresholds and category switches persist in the local profile's `modSettings/FS25_StorageWarnings.xml`; no alert history is saved. A reader could copy only an already computed snapshot whose `lastScanStats.farmId` equals the selected farm, label it as a local-player current alert snapshot, and withhold stale/remote-client state; it must not invoke `scan()` or imply the alerts are durable events. **Dedicated-server boundary:** `SW:update` returns before `scan()` whenever `g_localPlayer` is nil, so a server exporter cannot rely on a refreshed alert list there despite the descriptor's multiplayer flag. Its `loadMap` clears both the alerts and scan stats. Adding a general FarmHub page now would mostly duplicate native condition data while leaving the dedicated-server case unavailable. Defer a page until a live host/local snapshot can be demonstrated as meaningful and a separate server-safe data contract exists; no collector or installed game/export/page comparison has been made.

The threshold is a minimum **current stock value** for notification, not a per-unit commodity price. Modes 1/2 mean best-price month and green value. Its live `alertProductRows`, stock and profit tables are recalculated projections, not realized sales or profit. Older shared-list save XML can be migrated to the loading player's farm, so a farm-keyed runtime row does not prove an explicitly recorded historical owner. Farm Dashboard now has a **source-tested read-only page** for configured selected-farm product alerts and sowing reminders on land still owned by that farm. It caps both register scans at 300 and visible rows at 60, withholds foreign/stale fields and invalid settings, and shows an empty or pre-load register as limited rather than proving there are no alerts. It calls no alert setter, stock refresh or loan method. Three focused Lua/merge tests, two UI tests, nine catalog tests, typecheck, modDesc XML parse and Vite build passed. No private package, nonempty game/export/installed-page comparison or alert-trigger gameplay has been accepted; normal-set Ally loading is still blocked by RF StockGuard's DLC-version refusal.

- **Udder to Tank v1.0.0.0 source-tested reader, installed acceptance pending:** Lower-ranked `uncategorized/KM_82886_Udder_to_Tank.zip`, SHA-256 `A18854B49A44AE6110CE887D8A9F87EB9B1FB23C27FF9889AB8C1CA5158D638D`, stores `udderMilkAnimals` on managed animal clusters. - **Agro Realistic land-resale reader added in source after frozen V61:** The read-only collector requires server state and rejects a known version other than v1.0.0.0, reads the mod's in-memory farmland start periods, and resolves every farmland ID through current ownership before selecting the export farm. It rejects unknown ownership and impossible future/invalid history, caps scanning/rows, and computes only the source's current resale factor and elapsed periods since the **recorded** start. It does not call the mod's price-changing or save methods. The page explicitly warns that old-save migration can set the recorded start later than the true purchase and that a factor is not a confirmed sale payment. Focused Lua/merge tests cover owned, foreign and unowned land, source arithmetic, unknown owner, client withholding and invalid history; UI typecheck and 27-locale fallback checks pass. - **Agro Realistic zero-health animal timer is a second distinct source reading:** In the same exact ZIP, `scripts/animal_zero_health_loss.lua` persists `LeandroFarmRulesAnimalHealthLoss.timers` to save-local `AgroRealistic_animalHealth.xml`, keyed by husbandry unique ID and cluster ID, without a farm ID. Its server's hourly handler starts a counter when a positive animal cluster reaches zero health, increments it on subsequent hour changes, and at 24 counted hours requests the loss of half the cluster (rounded up), resetting or removing the timer. It removes a timer after recovery or when its cluster disappears; no historical loss ledger exists. The new read-only server collector joins each active timer to the exact current owned placeable/cluster and its current zero-health state, withholds if ownership cannot be resolved, caps traversal, and exports counted hours and current animals only. Its page explicitly states that the threshold is conditional, not a guaranteed loss or loss history. Focused exact-farm, foreign/orphan, recovery, client and ownership tests, typecheck, Vite build and 27-locale checks pass. This is source-only after frozen V61: no zero-health hour-change gameplay, private package, committed export or installed-page proof. A nonempty game test would need a safely staged zero-health owned animal cluster and the hourly event; do not infer it from the static timer fixture.

The land-resale history and zero-health timers above are the two distinct persisted readings found in this ZIP. This is exact-source classification, not installed coexistence or gameplay proof for the other rules.

## 2026-11-07 Ritter Fresh source lead

`gameplay/GH_rittermod__FS25_Fresh_FS25_Fresh.zip` declares v1.1.0.0-dev.1 and hashes to SHA-256 `C2182676DADAE4CA20926614F143C9EE13A8E5244A5088182452A108F64E20EF`. It has distinct save-local freshness batches and a loss log, beyond native Storage levels. `RmLossTracker:recordExpiration` runs on the server and writes a loss row with amount, fill type, game date, location, entity type, storage class and `farmId`; `RmFreshIO:saveLog` persists that row in `rm_FreshLog.xml` and `loadLog` restores it. The row's `value` is calculated at expiration from fill-type base price, seasonal factor and economy multiplier. It is an **estimated market value of expired goods**, not a recorded sale, debit or realized expense. The source starts with `container.farmId` and checks a live entity owner only when the container farm ID is zero, so an exact-farm filter can show the mod-recorded farm ID but cannot independently prove historical ownership or detect a stale nonzero owner. The global `statistics.totalExpired` is not farm-keyed and must not be presented as a farm total. Current container freshness needs a separate live-owner/reconciliation check before it can be shown as current farm stock.

Farm Dashboard now has a **source-tested read-only candidate** for the exact version: a detected Storage companion page scans no more than the newest 2,000 log entries, shows at most 40 valid rows for the selected farm, and labels counts/litres as the scanned window. It withholds ownerless/foreign rows, invalid dates/amounts, client-only state and an unready manager; a source `value` of zero is shown as an unavailable estimate. It never calls the mod's expiration, loading or aggregation methods. Five focused Lua/merge/UI tests, 17 catalog/presentation tests, UI typecheck and Vite build passed. No private package, nonempty game/export/installed-page comparison or expiration gameplay has been accepted. Ally normal-set loading remains blocked by RF StockGuard's DLC-version refusal.

**Current freshness needs a live ownership check:** The same exact ZIP exposes `RmFreshManager:getInventorySummary(farmId)` and `getExpiringWithin(hours, farmId)`, but both select current containers by the cached `container.farmId`. On save reconciliation, `registerContainer` replaces the identity and attaches `runtimeEntity` without refreshing that farm ID. A vehicle, bale or placeable could have changed owner after the cached value was recorded; the summary alone cannot prove present ownership. Object-storage entries use an abstract stored object as `runtimeEntity`, with the storage placeable's owner copied into metadata at registration. Farm Dashboard now has a **source-tested candidate** in the existing Ritter Fresh page: it resolves a normal container's current runtime owner, or matches an abstract object to the current owning storage placeable by unique ID and object reference, before including any batch. Unresolved owners are withheld and coverage is partial; the page distinguishes Ritter Fresh batch amounts from independently measured native Storage levels and labels expiry-disabled state. A focused stale-owner/stored-object Lua test, UI test, typecheck and Vite build pass. The existing loss log remains a historical mod-recorded farm attribution. No nonempty game/export/installed-page proof, live transfer test or private package is claimed, and normal-set Ally loading is still blocked by RF StockGuard's DLC-version refusal.

## 2026-11-07 lower-ranked vault source triage

- **Manure Ground is a terrain effect, not a separate application ledger:** Exact `gameplay/FS25_369912_FS25_manureGround.zip` v1.0.2.0 SHA-256 `2B3AB6C8AFA73E8782D9EE86A3D4523250452218D3C0DCB8C3E729B5DC5CCCDC` adds `ManureGroundDepositor` to spreaders. It tracks consumed manure and a transient field remainder during a work-area pass, then calls `DensityMapHeightUtil.tipToGroundAroundLine` for `MANURE_DIRTY`. The remainder is reset on load/delete and this ZIP has no saved per-farm application total. Ground piles and fill levels belong to the game terrain/storage state; the temporary remainder cannot be displayed as completed field coverage or used manure history. This source classification is not a gameplay or coexistence pass.

## 2026-11-07 lower-ranked Lua source scan

`2026-11-07-scan-vault-lua-signals.py` read Lua members of the existing 8,012-path vault inventory without changing the ZIPs. It scanned 1,803 Lua-bearing archives, recorded zero read errors, and capped five archives with more than 256 Lua members or a Lua member larger than 2 MB. The resulting source-signal queue is `Z:\Graham\agent-comms\farmhub-companion-acceptance\VAULT-LUA-SIGNALS-2026-11-07.json`. Its 402 rows scoring at least 10 are **review leads**, not 402 missing integrations or 402 distinct mods. A save-path or `farmId` string alone does not establish durable farm-owned data. The five capped archives are CoursePlay, two Fields of Stories ZIPs, and two Manual Paint ZIPs; their signal coverage is incomplete.

- **Customer Hay Orders is a shared queue without order ownership:** Three vault paths are byte-identical v0.9.9.3 ZIPs, SHA-256 `6DFC112404AC7E04A69B38D8B789CF07EC4A433C805E38CE0C417C0E4FCF0432`. Its `CHO.orders` contains useful accepted-order requirements, progress, deadline and reward, but each order has no farm ID. The action event transmits a `farmId` that `respondToOrder` does not bind to the accepted order; the server broadcasts the common order queue. A delivered bale's owner can determine the **payment farm** at completion, which does not retroactively identify the owner of every pending order. The source therefore supports a map-wide order board, not a selected-farm contract ledger. No farm-scoped companion page or gameplay acceptance is claimed.
- **Bank And Credit is a distinct farm-keyed loan register:** Exact `objects/KM_77828_Bank_And_Credit.zip` v1.1.0.0 hashes to SHA-256 `EF48ABBF420C06824CCF729F33079BA95273A6B0E40397A6D57DD4B1D7AAB6A2`. Its `BankRepository` persists `bankCredit.xml`; every `Loan` has a farm ID, type (annuity, bullet or revolving), original and remaining principal, interest rate, remaining duration, monthly payment and paid-off state. The source binds the repository at `g_currentMission.bankManager.repository` and exposes `getByFarm(farmId)`. `restAmount` means outstanding principal for annuity/bullet and drawn amount for revolving; `amount` is the original principal or revolving limit. The in-game `BankFrame` formats `interestRate` as a percent and leaves a revolving loan without a fixed monthly payment. This is a separate lender from the Realistic Farm Finance obligations page, and the two registers must never be merged as if they were one bank or added to native debt without explicit reconciliation. Farm Dashboard now has a **source-tested read-only candidate** for the export farm's active rows, with exact farm filtering, paid-off exclusion, server-only access, a 300-record scan bound and a 40-row display bound. Focused Lua/merge tests, TypeScript, Vite build, XML parse and targeted 27-locale key checks passed. No private package, nonempty game/export/installed-page comparison, repayment test or normal-set coexistence has been accepted for this ZIP. The general locale verifier still reports 44 pre-existing missing keys per language; this candidate added no further missing keys.
- **Real Tire Wear has wheel passports and farm-keyed spare stock:** Exact `uncategorized/KM_82753_Real_Tire_Wear.zip` v1.8.0.0 hashes to SHA-256 `CBDE5380F28185B18AA20F157F55960BABA9518BED59F7142E0FBFF857CC8E89`. Its server wheel state keeps a passport per serviceable tire with three tread bands, distance split by road/field/other, hours and repair count; live tire damage and air loss can differ from the stored passport until a snapshot. The extension writes passports into each vehicle's save XML. Separately, `RTWInventory.farms[farmId].stock` persists spare passports in `realTireWearInventory.xml`; the source moves removed tires into the current vehicle owner's stock. These readings are distinct from Variable Tire Pressure's current pressure mode and Punctured Tires' scheduled/active puncture state. Farm Dashboard now has a **source-tested read-only candidate** with separate mounted-wheel and spare-stock tables. It rechecks the vehicle's current farm owner, reads only the server, limits each vehicle to 24 wheels and the page to 400 scanned/80 shown mounted wheels, requires the inventory's loaded/current-mission state before reporting a spare count, and caps spare records at 128 scanned/40 shown. An unavailable inventory remains partial rather than becoming an empty farm stock; a saved passport is not proof of a completed repair or traction outcome. Focused collector/merge and UI tests pass, including foreign/stale owner exclusion and unavailable inventory; typecheck, XML parse and targeted 27-locale key checks pass. No private package, nonempty game/export/installed-page comparison or repair/traction gameplay acceptance is claimed yet.
- **Dedicated-page merge capacity:** The UI registry has 99 dedicated companion entries, but the app merge previously retained at most 80 page keys. A fully loaded export could therefore silently lose later pages. The bounded merge cap is now 128, with a source test that derives the current registry count and proves all registered pages survive; a second case proves an oversized 140-page payload remains capped. This is a source-level capacity correction, not an installed full-catalog acceptance result.

## 2026-11-08 Lease Livestock source lead

Exact `objects/KM_81709_Lease_Livestock.zip` v1.0.0.0 SHA-256 `E11DED0BCFCCFB845E32EFCCF3CFB5FF6C6500C7077CF79AEC9E3C4F0587C3BE` is the only Lease Livestock archive path in the current vault scan. This is a distinct animal-finance reading rather than another native animal count: `LL_AnimalClusterLeased.lua` stores and network-syncs `cluster.isLeased`, persists it as `#isLeased` in the native cluster save XML, and keeps leased clusters separate from owned clusters. `LL_LeaseLivestock:onPeriodChanged` charges each husbandry's owner farm `ceil(animal buy price at age 18 / 24) × current leased animals`; `addLease` charges the first period on acquisition. The saved flag is current lease status, **not** a payment or contract ledger, and the computed rate is a prospective period charge rather than proof of a debit. The exact source supports a read-only farm page that scans only server husbandries whose placeable `getOwnerFarmId()` equals the export farm, counts only flagged clusters, and labels the rate as an estimate. It must not call `addLease`, the event or animal-screen hooks. Farm Dashboard now has a **source-tested read-only candidate**: it rejects a known unreviewed mod version and requires the server husbandry register, selects only currently farm-owned pens, counts flagged leased clusters by subtype, and recomputes the mod's `ceil(price at age 18 / 24)` rate as a prospective per-period estimate. Missing prices or incomplete scans suppress the farm total. Focused farm-isolation/merge and UI farm-scope tests, typecheck, Vite build, XML parse and 27-locale checks passed. No nonempty game/export/installed-page comparison, period debit or normal-set compatibility proof exists. The local FS25 game wiki/extract used for the husbandry/cluster structure is version 1.20; the current Ally game is 1.25.1, so installed behavior remains a separate gate.

Its page shows only the pending aggregate and sale period; no individual sale, crop, fulfilled payment or current cash is invented. When the loaded register has no selected-farm row, it reports that absence without asserting a zero-valued receivable. Focused farm-isolation/invalid-state tests, TypeScript typecheck, direct Vite build, descriptor parse and all nine new keys in 27 locale files passed. The general i18n verifier still reports the same 44 missing keys per non-English locale that existed before this change; these new keys are present with English fallback text. No private package, nonempty game/export/installed-page parity or normal-set coexistence is accepted.

`scripts/TireLife.lua` separately saves per-vehicle, per-wheel-configuration wear lists in save-local `autoswitch_tires.xml` by `vehicle.uniqueId`. Its live `vehicles[vehicle]` holds the active wheel set; `pending[id]` holds loaded sets not yet applied to the vehicle. A wheel cut is encoded as wear plus 10 in the saved list, so the two values must be split before display. FarmHub now has a **source-tested read-only candidate** that reads only the active server module and exact v1.0.3.0, joins live and pending records to vehicles still owned by the export farm, rechecks current ownership, uses live wheel fields for the active set and saved lists for other sets, and bounds configurations, wheels and output rows. It never calls AutoSwitch's mutating `getState` or tire replacement methods. The page labels live versus saved readings and shown row count; inactive Use Your Tyres/disabled-module state, missing records, malformed values and scan truncation are explicit rather than converted to zero wear. Focused collector/merge and rendered-page tests, typecheck, Vite build, descriptor parse and 27-locale key checks passed. No private package, nonempty game/export/installed-page parity, tire replacement or normal-set coexistence is accepted. The mod's `AS_Overdraft:getLimit(farmId)` derives a borrowing limit from native asset values, so that figure needs an estimate label and is not a saved debt ledger. `AutoSwitch.lua` loads these modules but also declares that its tire-wear module blocks Real Physics Tire Wear; compatibility and any overlap with FarmHub's separate Real Tire Wear page need an explicit test. The remaining AutoSwitch modules and the effects of its finance/vehicle hooks require separate review before any broad suite claim.

## 2026-11-08 Kringstad Sugar Beet Piler farm ledger candidate

Exact `uncategorized/KM_82896_Kringstad_Ironworks_Sugar_Beet_Piler.zip` v1.1.0.0 SHA-256 `9370ADC25829010D72E480FF9C170B72FD4C7716D9715156A7E7E2361512254F` is a placeable with a distinct farm-keyed obligation. `TareDirtManagement.lua` saves `spec.outstandingByFarm[farmId]` and `spec.pendingBatches` under each piler's native placeable save XML. The on-site trigger reports the selected farm's outstanding litres. A public, unowned piler can hold different balances for multiple delivering farms, so filtering only by piler owner would lose valid obligations; the stored farm IDs are the data boundary. Source also states that newly generated tare may be attributed to the piler owner or current farm when delivery attribution is unavailable. At day change the mod caps disposal by physical tare, may use a fallback when no delivery ledger exists, and books any fee to native farm money. The outstanding amount, pending generation and configured fee rate are **not** a guaranteed future charge or a ledger of charged fees.

FarmHub now has a **source-tested read-only candidate** for exact v1.1.0.0. It scans loaded server piler specializations with hard placeable/piler/batch limits, reads only the selected farm's keyed outstanding and pending amounts even on shared public pilers, and withholds farm totals on malformed or truncated state. It does not query global physical tare as a farm amount, call the piler's mutating debt/removal/day-end methods or infer a fee debit. A full page labels the fee as a configured per-litre rate. Focused public/owned/foreign-farm and invalid-state collector/merge tests, rendered-page tests, typecheck, Vite build, descriptor parse and 27-locale key checks passed. There is no private package, in-game nonempty tare event, fresh committed export, installed-page parity or normal-set compatibility proof. The historical 65-entry matrix remains unchanged.

The historical B2 result was an **empty task list**, so this exact archive has no nonempty task creation, completion, committed-export or installed-list parity pass. It is distinct from Field To-Do List, which has different field-plan and orphan-task provenance rules. This archive is not a newly missing integration.
- Exact `uncategorized/FS25_359391_Realistic_Shopping.zip` v1.0.1.0 SHA-256 `EF120D088B2F5DD0C0CE3BBC87341910D325A84AD19D9D3AEE9DFEFE869B65D8` has a **genuine pending-delivery order register**. `RS.orders` stores order ID, item name, quoted price, delivery fee, ordered/due game minutes, status, farm ID and lease/Hire Purchasing flags. `saveSettingsAndOrders` writes only pending valid orders to save-local `realisticShopping` XML; delivery processing and HUD use the live register. This is more than native purchased-vehicle data, but it is an order queue, not a completed purchase ledger or proof of delivery. The source's load path defaults a missing saved `#farmId` to farm 1, and several order constructors likewise default an absent farm ID to 1. A safe FarmHub farm-1 reader therefore needs original farm-ID provenance, not merely `order.farmId == 1` in the loaded table; export must withhold legacy/ambiguous rows. Hire Purchasing terms duplicated inside order purchase data must not be counted as another finance deal. No collector or exact-version game/export/page/compatibility acceptance is claimed yet.

The order provenance problem remains after a deeper source trace. The mod's `saveSettingsAndOrders` writes `#farmId` for every pending row, but its `loadSettingsAndOrders` first replaces a missing legacy attribute with `1`; any subsequent save can therefore write a *new explicit* `#farmId=1` for an originally unattributed order. The `makeOrderSignature` function also defaults missing farm IDs to 1, and the general shop constructor gets its ID from `getControllerFarmId`, which returns 1 when the controller lacks a farm reference. The saved XML attribute or signature alone cannot recover original farm-1 ownership. Farm IDs above 1 remain distinguishable from this fallback, but a page that works only for those farms would leave the normal farm-1 path unproved. No general selected-farm collector should be added until the third-party source supplies explicit ownership provenance or a separately verified safe join; a controlled new-order fixture could prove only that fixture. This is a source-level ownership limitation, not a FarmHub failure or an order-delivery gameplay verdict.

## 2026-11-08 Tree Mapper, Grass Collection and Impact Tree Feller source check

- `gameplay/FS25_373292_Tree_Mapper.zip` and `uncategorized/KM_82792_Tree_Mapper.zip` are byte-identical v1.0.0.0 archives, SHA-256 `33FE99D67E0448BCC218EAFAE16DD737542FB4BA77801D932D5F1912E54F6209`. Its server-side `TreeIndex` scans all world trees for a map view. `TreeMapper.lua` persists only x/y/z coordinates of removed trees in save-local `treeMapper.xml`; no removal row carries farm ID or a durable farmland owner. The removal request checks the requesting farm's land for ordinary multiplayer users, while admins may remove more broadly. That permission check cannot turn the historical coordinate list or current world-tree inventory into selected-farm forestry work. - Exact `uncategorized/FS25_369338_Grass_Collection.zip` v1.0.0.0 SHA-256 `DC68B8C232A219EB9C70710F07996E090667511513262014DA31DAB5AA9BBD75` saves `currentFill`, active/auto flags and capacity/pickup upgrade tiers in save-local `grassCollection.xml` (legacy `bareHandsHarvest.xml` fallback). The state is a player-carried, tiny grass buffer and upgrade controls, with no player or farm ID in the saved record. Unloading into a native trigger and upgrade spending use the current player's farm ID, but that does not establish ownership of the saved carry state across players. - Exact `uncategorized/FS25_368812_Impact_Tree_Feller_-_Impact_Damage.zip` v1.0.0.5 SHA-256 `FF4A86BC489771AABB8EFBFA4C77D5A07AF2ED6DD90154944F79A82458BBABD1` has one distinct **pending wood-chip pallet queue**. When its optional pallet mode cannot create a pallet, it queues litres, a source-vehicle farm ID, position and direction; the server writes those entries to save-local `impactTreeFeller` XML on a game save and retries while a slot is available. This is a backlog, not a record of all felled trees, realized wood chips or completed pallets. Source `loadSettings` defaults a missing queued `#farmId` to farm 1, and `queueWoodChipPallet` likewise defaults a missing task farm to 1; the vehicle farm helper may fall back to active farm for leased/contract vehicles. A safe selected-farm reader must prove original farm provenance and distinguish a pending queue from already spawned native pallets. The master felling switch resets off on load; other persisted options are save-wide settings. Given the rare pallet-limit state and ambiguous legacy ownership, this is a conditional candidate, not an accepted farm page or runtime compatibility pass.

## 2026-11-08 Flock Camera source candidate

Exact `uncategorized/KM_82460_Flock_Camera.zip` v1.0.0.0 SHA-256 `969A285A9EFB8F793A0DB7D7362B79C1637BE515C22481DE7784C6D03D78DB5D` registers a placeable specialization with mod-owned `ledMode`, `alertMode`, fallen/repair state and a capped 30-entry event history. The placeable calls the specialization's namespaced `loadFromXMLFile`/`saveToXMLFile`, so the state is retained inside native placeable XML rather than a separate modSettings file. Its camera control requires the player's farm ID to equal the placeable owner; vehicle detection records text such as the vehicle name and rounded speed and can alert the owner farm. `addFlockEvent` prepends game day and time to new event text, while XML stores that text rather than a separate timestamp field. FarmHub now has a **source-tested read-only owned-placeable candidate**: it requires the reviewed version and specialization key, server-side ready state, current `getOwnerFarmId()`, valid modes and bounded camera/event scans. The page shows the mod's recorded text but does not calculate event age or treat detection speed as a violation; the mod is not a video recording or livestock monitoring camera. Focused Lua and rendered-page tests, detector/catalog parity, TypeScript typecheck, Vite build, descriptor parse and 27-locale key checks passed. Non-English locale keys carry English fallback text. No private package, nonempty game/export/installed-page comparison or normal-set compatibility is established yet.

## 2026-11-08 TH Design Kit source classification

The six `uncategorized/ITCH_*_FS25_0_THDesignKit.zip` queue paths are the same v1.1.0.0 archive, SHA-256 `CC99E84FC96AF05577744C98F895B2D768694B360A6A47222C8EA1E21A7B1C79` (duplicate-byte proof recorded above). In the reviewed source, changing a placed object's appearance calls native `placeable:setConfigurations`, updates native `boughtConfigurations`, raises native configuration load events and charges `MoneyType.PROPERTY_MAINTENANCE` to the acting/owner farm when a design is purchased. The event carries a farm ID for authorization/payment, but the result lives in the native placeable configuration and finance state; the mod does not retain a separate selected-farm design history or purchase ledger. Its extra density-map manager writes map layers, not an individual farm's work. This is a useful in-game customization mod with **native-state overlap**, not six missing FarmHub companion pages. Exact-version installed coexistence and visual design parity remain untested.
